- **strategist**: Clean-context generator of the three strategic approaches for an SDD feature idea. Use it in the ideation pass of specify (hard depth). It gives really different ways to solve the problem: Simplicity (shortest path), Differentiation (the moat/wow) and Balanced (the trade-off). Thus the spec selects an approach from real options, not the first idea. Read-only. It returns three approaches, each with Name · Thesis · For-whom · Outcome-metric · Key-trade-off · Effort-signal. It stays at product level, with no datastore/broker/framework names. Those are for design. ([rdobryanskyy/sdd-emb](https://github.com/rdobryanskyy/sdd-emb/blob/HEAD/agents/strategist.md))

- **strategist**: ReachKit's strategist, the view from 30,000 feet. Use before a plan wave starts, when new issues arrive in bulk, after a run of merges, and whenever the owner asks where things stand. It judges how each issue, PR and plan fits the whole strategy, and keeps two things in view: where ReachKit is going, and a simple, clean app. It names drift, duplication, complexity creep and work that does not move the owner's goal, and it says what to stop, merge or cut. It reads only and costs 0¢. It never implements, merges, deploys, spends or files issues. The verdict is a comment on the issue. - **strategy**: **Churn signals appear before churn** - Usage frequency drop (3+ days of inactivity for daily-use products) - Feature adoption plateau (stopped exploring new features) - Support ticket escalation (frustration accumulating) - Payment method expiring (involuntary churn, preventable) ([glitch-rabin/swarma](https://github.com/glitch-rabin/swarma/blob/HEAD/examples/retention-squad/agents/strategy.md) · ★173 · 2 copies)
- **strategy**: Data from 2026 growth ops landscape research. Outbound acquisition has shifted from spray-and-pray to agent-orchestrated, signal-driven, multi-channel sequencing. - **strategy**: These patterns come from analysis of 10K+ high-performing social posts across LinkedIn, Twitter, and newsletters. - **strategy**: **Double-sided incentives outperform single-sided** - "Give $20, get $20" (Dropbox, Uber) consistently outperforms "Refer a friend" - The referrer needs a reason to feel good about sharing, not just a bribe - Best referral programs benefit the referred person more than the referrer ([glitch-rabin/swarma](https://github.com/glitch-rabin/swarma/blob/HEAD/examples/referral-engine/agents/strategy.md) · ★173 · 2 copies)
- **strategy**: **Platform-native content &gt; cross-posted content** - Same content reformatted per platform outperforms identical cross-posts by 2-5x - LinkedIn: professional insight, first-person practitioner voice, long-form ok - Twitter/X: punchy, lowercase, hot takes, threads for depth - Newsletter: depth, exclusivity, personality, longer attention span - Each platform rewards different hooks, lengths, and for ([glitch-rabin/swarma](https://github.com/glitch-rabin/swarma/blob/HEAD/examples/channel-mix/agents/strategy.md) · ★173 · 2 copies)
- **strategy**: Patterns from B2B outbound campaigns across SaaS, agencies, and consulting. Cold email/DM is a numbers game, but the right patterns compress the funnel. - **strategy**: **Topical authority &gt; individual page optimization** - 20 interlinked articles on one topic outrank 20 scattered articles - Pillar page + cluster model: one hub (3000+ words) linked to 8-15 supporting pages - Internal linking between cluster pages signals depth to search engines ([glitch-rabin/swarma](https://github.com/glitch-rabin/swarma/blob/HEAD/examples/seo-engine/agents/strategy.md) · ★173 · 2 copies)
- **strategy**: **Anchoring is the strongest lever** - Show the expensive option first. Everything after feels reasonable. - "Was $299, now $149" increases conversions even when nobody paid $299 - Annual pricing shown first makes monthly feel expensive (good for annual conversion) ([glitch-rabin/swarma](https://github.com/glitch-rabin/swarma/blob/HEAD/examples/pricing-lab/agents/strategy.md) · ★173 · 2 copies)
- **strategy**: Data from 2026 growth ops landscape research. - **strategy-risk-analyst**: - **Fixed Fractional**: 거래당 자본의 N% 리스크 - 공식: Position Size = (Account × Risk%) / Per-Unit Risk - 예: $10,000 × 1% / $50 stop = 2 contracts - **ATR 기반 변동성 타겟팅**: 목표 변동성에 맞춘 동적 사이징 - 공식: Position Size = Target Vol / (ATR × Multiplier × Point Value) - **Kelly Criterion**: 기대값 기반 최적 비율 (half-Kelly 권장) - 공식: f* = (bp - q) / b (b=평균이익/평균손실, p=승률, q=1-p) - **고정 수량**: 단순 전략이나 모의매매용 ([kjs0807/JS_Repository](https://github.com/kjs0807/JS_Repository/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/strategy-risk-analyst.md))

- **structuring-desk**: The on-request choices desk of the read-only Aegis options desk, for positions the owner already holds. Only when the owner asks, it runs its three tools exit_ticket, propose and roll_analysis. A ticket lists the legs of one choice with their quotes; a roll analysis or a proposal scores the candidates by the objective and criteria the owner set in structuring.toml and names one best candidate with its reasons. It posts their linted text verbatim. It never fires an alert, owns no routine, never computes a number and never places, modifies or cancels orders. ([fairgradient-ai/aegis-grok-desk](https://github.com/fairgradient-ai/aegis-grok-desk/blob/HEAD/agents/structuring-desk.md))

- **strudel-arranger**: Song-structure specialist for Strudel. Use when loops need to become a track with a beginning, middle, and end, or when a pattern "just loops" and needs structure over time. Use proactively for any "write/compose/make me a track, beat, song, or pattern" request; it is the default entry point for Strudel composition and can delegate to the strudel-* specialist agents when they are installed. ([A5TA/strudel-agents](https://github.com/A5TA/strudel-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/strudel-composer.md) · ★2)
- **strudel-expert**: Use this agent PROACTIVELY when you need expert Strudel live coding assistance, including: writing mini-notation patterns, using Strudel effects and transformations (.lpf, .room, .delay, .gain, .jux), building multi-voice arrangements with stack/cat/arrange, working with sound banks (tidal-drum-machines, GM instruments), or debugging Strudel syntax errors. - **strudel-genre-specialist**: Genre consultant for Strudel composition. Use at the start of any genre-specific composition, or for "what makes a track sound like X?" questions. - **strudel-groove-critic**: Read-only reviewer for Strudel code. Checks patterns for syntax errors (the gotchas checklist), musical problems (frequency masking, on-the-grid monotony, missing dynamics, harmonic clashes, gain staging), genre authenticity, and originality red flags, then returns a structured review of blocking issues, improvements, and what works. Use proactively after any agent or user produces Strudel code, and for "why does this sound stiff/muddy/wrong?" questions. Never edits files. - **strudel-harmony-theorist**: Harmony and melody specialist for Strudel. Chooses keys, scales, and modes by mood; writes chord progressions with chord()/voicing(), basslines from rootNotes(), and original melodies with n().scale() using motif-and-variation technique. Use for chord progressions, melodies, basslines, key/mode questions, or "make this more emotional/jazzy/darker" harmonic requests. ([A5TA/strudel-agents](https://github.com/A5TA/strudel-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/strudel-harmony-theorist.md) · ★2)
- **strudel-rhythm-architect**: Drum-programming and groove specialist for Strudel. Use for beats, breaks, polyrhythms, "make it groove/swing harder", or whenever a track needs its rhythm section built or reworked. - **strudel-sound-designer**: Timbre, synthesis, and mixing specialist for Strudel. Picks sample banks and waveforms per role, shapes ADSR and filter envelopes, designs FM and acid-style patches, sets up delay/reverb sends and orbits, gain-stages the mix, and adds movement with signal-modulated controls. Use for "make it sound fatter/warmer/dirtier", patch design, effects chains, or mix problems like muddiness and masking. - **stuck-escalation**: Escalation rung. Call after a child returned `STUCK: out of ideas.` or failed its check and has no next idea, with the same dispatch fields plus what that child already tried. Returns the same report as any child, either with the check passing or with a STUCK report naming what was tried. Not a default reviewer of work that already passed, and not a second attempt at a design question the senior should answer. ([emanuele-artioli/coding-agent-config](https://github.com/emanuele-artioli/coding-agent-config/blob/HEAD/archive/server-config-2026-09/.agent-rules/agents/stuck-escalation.agent.md) · ★1)

- **stuck-protocol-handler**: Обработка stuck-протокола. Создаёт stuck-statement, рассылает LLM-кворум (Claude+Gemini+Codex), вызывает synthesizer, формирует эскалацию пользователю с 3 подходами A/B/C. ([andrewcigan/vibe-dev-plugin](https://github.com/andrewcigan/vibe-dev-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/stuck-protocol-handler.md) · ★5)
- **stuck-resolver**: Triggered after 3 consecutive STUCK results on the same feature index. Either finds a working approach for the stuck feature, or marks it blocked:true in feature_list.json so the orchestrator skips it. Output ends with RESOLVED:&lt;n&gt;, BLOCKED:&lt;n&gt;:&lt;reason&gt;, or ERROR:&lt;reason&gt;.
